General Chairs’ Welcome
It is our pleasure to welcome you to the 30th ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data
Mining---KDD’2024 in Barcelona, Catalonia. This year’s conference continues its tradition of being the
premier forum for presentation of research results and experience reports on knowledge discovery, data
science, and machine learning. The mission of the conference is to provide the best venue for
advancement, education, and adoption of the “science” of knowledge discovery and machine learning
from all types of data; to share novel methodologies that fulfill the needs of heterogeneous applications
and environments and identify new directions for future research and development. These ideas have the
potential to shape and impact our society and environment, becoming particularly important with the
emergence of AI in all fields. So, KDD provides researchers and practitioners a unique opportunity to
share their perspectives with others interested in various aspects of data science and machine learning.
KDD’24 has a program of three keynotes (Sanjeev Arora, Tanya Berger-Wolf and Xihong Lin), one panel
on generative AI, 411 research track papers, 151 applied data science (ADS) track papers and eight invited
talks, 30 workshops, 34 tutorials (nine of them hands-on), nine special days (one online for India), and
three KDD cups. We have introduced two new special days, one in Responsible AI and another in
European Data Science given the location of the conference. We also added one extra poster session to
have more time for posters presentations. For second time we used Openreview for the research and ADS
tracks with the goal to further improve the review quality and facilitate the interaction between reviewers
and authors. We hope that you will find this program interesting and thought-provoking and that the
conference will provide you with a valuable opportunity to share ideas with other researchers and
practitioners from institutions around the world.
Putting together KDD’24 was a team effort. We first thank all the authors for providing the content of the
technical program. We thank our program committee chairs (Research: Flavio Chierichetti, Danai Koutra,
and Ravi Kumar; ADS: Rajesh Parekh, Claudia Perlich, and Shipeng Yu) and our dedicated senior and
regular program committee members for their efforts in providing high-quality and timely reviews. We
are grateful to our 60 organizing committee members who worked very hard at countless tasks: reviewing
papers and workshop/tutorial proposals, inviting speakers, organizing special days/panels, selecting best
papers, selecting travel awards, handling local arrangements, organizing posters, proceedings,
undergraduate and PhD consortiums and the KDD cup, publicizing the conference on social media and the
Internet, budgeting, and handling registrations. Special shoutout to the sponsorship committee who raised
sponsorships tirelessly to support the conference. Finally, we thank our generous corporate sponsors,
ACM’s SIGKDD (especially Wei Wang’s leadership), and the organizational support of Executivevents.
Finally, KDD is rarely outside North America, so Barcelona, the capital of the autonomous community of
Catalonia, well known for its history, architecture, culture, and great food, it’s a special outlier this year.
So, we hope that you enjoy one of the most visited European cities, that thanks to its technological
neighborhood, 22@, to which the conference venue belongs, has also become one of the European data
science hubs.

Ricardo Baeza-Yates

Francesco Bonchi

General Co-Chair
EAI, Northeastern University
Silicon Valley, CA, USA &
Universitat Pompeu Fabra, Barcelona, Spain

General Co-Chair
CENTAI, Torino, Italy &
EURECAT, Barcelona, Spain

iii

PC Chairs’ Welcome
It is our great pleasure to welcome you to the 2024 ACM SIGKDD International Conference on
Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining in Barcelona, Spain. This year’s conference continues its
tradition of being the premier forum for presentation of research results and experience reports on
leading issues in Data Mining as well as in Data Science and Machine Learning. The large attendance of
the KDD conference, and the high impact of the KDD publications, give researchers and practitioners a
unique opportunity to share novel perspectives. The call for papers attracted submissions from Asia,
the Americas, Australia, Europe, and Africa. We received 2046 papers in the Research Track and 738
papers in the ADS (Applied Data Science) Track and we accepted 411 and 151 respectively.
This year the KDD conference continued with the reviewing process using the OpenReview system. For
the Research track accepted papers, we will make the reviews, the handling Area Chair (AC), and the
Senior Area Chair (SAC) public. We expect that the KDD community will embrace this reviewing
paradigm in the future and make the entire reviewing discussions open.
We extend our thanks to the SACs/ACs and the Program Committee members (21 SACs, 139 ACs, and
2619 reviewers for the Research Track; 67 and 469 for the ADS track) for their very hard and
conscientious work. It takes a lot of effort to produce a technical program on the order of KDD, but
none is as important as the effort of the reviewers. Their work is reflected in a very interesting and
strong technical program. 30 Research Track PC members and 14 ADS Track PC members were chosen
for the Best Reviewer Award in recognition of their work. We would also like to thank the entire
KDD24 organizing committee, and especially the General Chairs, Ricardo Baeza-Yates and Francesco
Bonchi and the Chair of the ACM Special Interest Group on Knowledge Discovery in Data (SIGKDD),
Wei Wang, for their leadership and confidence. We hope that you will find this year’s KDD program
interesting and thought-provoking. As KDD remains in the forefront of the intersection of data
analysis, data mining, and machine learning, an area of ever increasing importance, we hope that this
year’s conference will provide you with a valuable opportunity to share ideas with other researchers
and practitioners from institutions around the world.

Research Track Program Committee Chairs

Flavio Chierichetti, Danai Koutra, Ravi Kumar
Applied Data Science Track Program Committee Chairs

Rajesh Parekh, Claudia Perlich, Shipeng Yu
